Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a systematics algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 110. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 758/5.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by systematics generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 110, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 758. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the systematics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 478. The moduli space has dimension at most 958 with 3 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 478 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the systematics structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 958. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
